24:     let conn = Connection::open(db).unwrap();

28:         .unwrap_or(0);

58: fn table_exists(db: &str, table: &str) -> bool {
59:     use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags};
60: 
61:     if !std::path::Path::new(db).exists() {
62:         return false;
63:     }
64: 
65:     let conn = match Connection::open_with_flags(
66:         db,
67:         OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_NO_MUTEX,
68:     ) {
69:         Ok(c) => c,
70:         Err(_) => return false,
71:     };
72: 
73:     conn.query_row(
74:         "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?1 LIMIT 1",
75:         [table],
76:         |_r| Ok(()),
77:     )
78:     .is_ok()
79: }

408:         Some("emit-bias-artifact") => {
409:             use deterministic_ai_kernel::workflow::contract::StepKind;
410:             use deterministic_ai_kernel::workflow::semantic::bias::SemanticBias;
411:             use serde_json::json;
412: 
413:             fn parse_step_kind(s: &str) -> Option<StepKind> {
414:                 match s {
415:                     "TightenPlannerPrompt" => Some(StepKind::TightenPlannerPrompt),
416:                     "NormalizePlannerOutput" => Some(StepKind::NormalizePlannerOutput),
417:                     "AddLlmFallbackHandling" => Some(StepKind::AddLlmFallbackHandling),
418:                     "AddPlannerTestCoverage" => Some(StepKind::AddPlannerTestCoverage),
419:                     "ValidatePlannerOutput" => Some(StepKind::ValidatePlannerOutput),
420:                     "AnalyzeTask" => Some(StepKind::AnalyzeTask),
421:                     "PlanExecution" => Some(StepKind::PlanExecution),
422:                     "ExecuteChanges" => Some(StepKind::ExecuteChanges),
423:                     "ReadRepository" => Some(StepKind::ReadRepository),
424:                     "LocateBug" => Some(StepKind::LocateBug),
425:                     "PatchCode" => Some(StepKind::PatchCode),
426:                     "RunTests" => Some(StepKind::RunTests),
427:                     "ValidatePatch" => Some(StepKind::ValidatePatch),
428:                     _ => None,
429:                 }
430:             }
431: 
432:             let task_id = args.get(2).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
433:             let step_id = args.get(3).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
434:             if task_id.trim().is_empty() || step_id.trim().is_empty() {
435:                 eprintln!("usage: emit-bias-artifact <task_id> <step_id> <step_kind>...");
436:                 std::process::exit(1);
437:             }
438: 
439:             let raw_domain: Vec<String> = args.iter().skip(4).cloned().collect();
440:             if raw_domain.is_empty() {
441:                 eprintln!("usage: emit-bias-artifact <task_id> <step_id> <step_kind>...");
442:                 std::process::exit(1);
443:             }
444: 
445:             let mut domain: Vec<StepKind> = Vec::new();
446:             for raw in &raw_domain {
447:                 match parse_step_kind(raw) {
448:                     Some(kind) => domain.push(kind),
449:                     None => {
450:                         eprintln!("unknown step kind: {raw}");
451:                         std::process::exit(2);
452:                     }
453:                 }
454:             }
455: 
456:             let bias = SemanticBias::neutral_for(&domain);
457:             let payload = json!({
458:                 "lines": bias.explain_lines(),
459:                 "version": bias.version,
460:                 "seed": bias.seed,
461:                 "preferred": bias.preferred.iter().map(|k| format!("{:?}", k)).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
462:                 "weights": bias.weights,
463:             });
464: 
465:             let bus = deterministic_ai_kernel::event_bus::EventBus::new(db).unwrap();
466:             match bus.append_semantic_artifact(&task_id, &step_id, 0, "semantic_bias_v1", &payload)
467:             {
468:                 Ok(()) => {
469:                     println!("EMIT_BIAS_ARTIFACT_OK[id]{}[id]{}", task_id, step_id);
470:                 }
471:                 Err(e) => {
472:                     eprintln!("emit-bias-artifact failed: {e}");
473:                     std::process::exit(1);
474:                 }
475:             }
476:             return;
477:         }
478:

491:                     "PlanExecution" => Some(StepKind::PlanExecution),
492:                     "ExecuteChanges" => Some(StepKind::ExecuteChanges),

497:                     "ValidatePatch" => Some(StepKind::ValidatePatch),

821:             if role.trim().is_empty() || text.trim().is_empty() {
822:                 eprintln!("usage: dak prompt <role> [id]your text here[id]");
823:                 std::process::exit(1);

826:                 Ok(response) => println!("{}", response),

834:         Some("gateway-stdin") => {
835:             use std::io::Read;
836: 
837:             let mut input = String::new();
838:             std::io::stdin().read_to_string(&mut input).unwrap();
839: 
840:             if input.trim().is_empty() {
841:                 eprintln!("gateway-stdin: empty stdin");
842:                 std::process::exit(1);
843:             }
844: 
845:             let parsed: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(&input) {
846:                 Ok(v) => v,
847:                 Err(e) => {
848:                     eprintln!("gateway-stdin: invalid json: {}", e);
849:                     std::process::exit(1);
850:                 }
851:             };
852: 
853:             println!(
854:                 "{}",
855:                 serde_json::to_string(&parsed).unwrap_or_else(|e| {
856:                     eprintln!("Serialization failed: {e}");
857:                     std::process::exit(1);
858:                 })
859:             );
860:             return;
861:         }
862:         Some("pipeline-run") => {
863:             let mut task_id: Option<String> = None;
864:             let mut payload: Option<String> = None;
865:             let mut seed: u64 = 42;
866:             let mut as_json = false;
867:             let mut i = 2usize;
868:             while i < args.len() {
869:                 match args[i].as_str() {
870:                     "--task-id" => {
871:                         i += 1;
872:                         task_id = args.get(i).cloned();
873:                     }
874:                     "--payload" => {
875:                         i += 1;
876:                         payload = args.get(i).cloned();
877:                     }
878:                     "--seed" => {
879:                         i += 1;
880:                         seed = args.get(i).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(42);
881:                     }
882:                     "--json" => {
883:                         as_json = true;
884:                     }
885:                     other => {
886:                         eprintln!("unknown arg: {other}");
887:                         std::process::exit(1);
888:                     }
889:                 }
890:                 i += 1;
891:             }
892:             if task_id.is_some() == payload.is_some() {
893:                 eprintln!("usage: pipeline-run --task-id <id> [--seed <u64>] [--json]");
894:                 eprintln!("   or: pipeline-run --payload [id]...[id] [--seed <u64>] [--json]");
895:                 std::process::exit(1);
896:             }
897:             let resolved = if let Some(p) = payload {
898:                 p
899:             } else {
900:                 let conn = rusqlite::Connection::open(db).unwrap();
901:                 let id = task_id.unwrap_or_else(|| {
902:                     eprintln!("task_id is missing");
903:                     std::process::exit(1);
904:                 });
905:                 conn.query_row(
906:                     "SELECT input_representation FROM semantic_bias_artifacts WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY artifact_id DESC LIMIT 1",
907:                     [&id], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0)
908:                 ).unwrap_or_else(|_| { eprintln!("pipeline-run: no payload for task_id={id}"); std::process::exit(1); })
909:             };
910:             let report = match deterministic_ai_kernel::planner_pipeline::build_plan_and_publish(
911:                 &resolved, seed, db,
912:             ) {

67:         !self.capsule_id.is_empty() && !self.execution_id.is_empty() && !self.created_at.is_empty()

1: //! Unified error model for the Execution Kernel.
2: //!
3: //! All kernel-internal errors use `KernelError` instead of `anyhow::Error`.
4: //! This enforces structured error categorization across the kernel boundary.
5: 
6: use std::fmt;
7: 
8: /// Top-level error type for the Execution Kernel.
9: ///
10: /// Each variant corresponds to a distinct failure domain,
11: /// enabling structured error handling and deterministic diagnostics.
12: #[derive(Debug)]
13: pub enum KernelError {
14:     /// The ExecSpec or PrimitiveSpec is malformed, missing fields, or structurally invalid.
15:     Specification(SpecificationError),
16: 
17:     /// Invariant validation failed (hash mismatch, dependency violation, capability constraint).
18:     Validation(ValidationError),
19: 
20:     /// Runtime execution of a primitive failed.
21:     Execution(ExecutionError),
22: 
23:     /// A provider (filesystem, LLM, etc.) returned an error.
24:     Provider(ProviderError),
25: 
26:     /// Reconstruction from event log detected inconsistency.
27:     Reconstruction(ReconstructionError),
28: }
29: 
30: // ── Specification Errors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
31: 
32: #[derive(Debug)]
33: pub enum SpecificationError {
34:     /// ExecSpec JSON could not be parsed.
35:     MalformedSpec { detail: String },
36: 
37:     /// A referenced step_id does not exist in the spec.
38:     MissingStep { step_id: String },
39: 
40:     /// A dependency references a step that does not exist.
41:     InvalidDependency {
42:         step_id: String,
43:         missing_dep: String,
44:     },
45: 
46:     /// Spec version is unsupported.
47:     UnsupportedVersion { version: u32 },
48: 
49:     /// PrimitiveSpec is missing on a step that requires it.
50:     MissingPrimitive { step_id: String },
51: }
52: 
53: // ── Validation Errors ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
54: 
55: #[derive(Debug)]
56: pub enum ValidationError {
57:     /// spec_id does not match calculated hash.
58:     HashMismatch { spec_id: String, calculated: String },
59: 
60:     /// A dependency ordering violation was detected.
61:     DependencyViolation {
62:         step_id: String,
63:         parent_id: String,
64:         detail: String,
65:     },
66: 
67:     /// Worker capability does not match step requirement.
68:     CapabilityMismatch {
69:         step_id: String,
70:         worker_id: String,
71:         required: String,
72:         actual: String,
73:     },
74: 
75:     /// An event type is not recognized.
76:     UnknownEventType { event_type: String },
77: 
78:     /// A transition rule is invalid.
79:     InvalidTransition {
80:         from: String,
81:         to: String,
82:         reason: String,
83:     },
84: }
85: 
86: // ── Execution Errors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
87: 
88: #[derive(Debug)]
89: pub enum ExecutionError {
90:     /// The primitive could not be started.
91:     PrimitiveStartFailed { step_id: String, reason: String },
92: 
93:     /// The primitive timed out.
94:     PrimitiveTimeout { step_id: String, timeout_secs: u64 },
95: 
96:     /// The lease expired before completion.
97:     LeaseExpired { step_id: String, lease_id: String },
98: 
99:     /// Double-commit was attempted.
100:     DoubleCommit { step_id: String },
101: 
102:     /// Worker was rejected (stale claim).
103:     StaleClaim { step_id: String, worker_id: String },
104: }
105: 
106: // ── Provider Errors ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
107: 
108: #[derive(Debug)]
109: pub enum ProviderError {
110:     /// Filesystem provider error.
111:     Filesystem {
112:         operation: String,
113:         path: String,
114:         detail: String,
115:     },
116: 
117:     /// LLM provider error.
118:     Llm { prompt_hash: String, detail: String },
119: 
120:     /// Database / persistence error.
121:     Persistence { operation: String, detail: String },
122: 
123:     /// Provider not registered.
124:     NotRegistered { provider_type: String },
125: }
126: 
127: // ── Reconstruction Errors ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
128: 
129: #[derive(Debug)]
130: pub enum ReconstructionError {
131:     /// Event log is corrupted or has gaps.
132:     CorruptedEventLog { task_id: String, detail: String },
133: 
134:     /// Replay produced different state than original execution.
135:     ReplayDivergence { task_id: String, detail: String },
136: 
137:     /// Effect ledger has orphaned reservations.
138:     OrphanedEffects { task_id: String, count: u64 },
139: 
140:     /// Event ordering violation in causal unit.
141:     CausalOrderViolation { unit_id: i64, detail: String },
142: }
143: 
144: // ── Display implementations ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
145: 
146: impl fmt::Display for KernelError {
147:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
148:         match self {
149:             Self::Specification(e) => write!(f, "specification error: {}", e),
150:             Self::Validation(e) => write!(f, "validation error: {}", e),
151:             Self::Execution(e) => write!(f, "execution error: {}", e),
152:             Self::Provider(e) => write!(f, "provider error: {}", e),
153:             Self::Reconstruction(e) => write!(f, "reconstruction error: {}", e),
154:         }
155:     }
156: }
157: 
158: impl fmt::Display for SpecificationError {
159:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
160:         match self {
161:             Self::MalformedSpec { detail } => write!(f, "malformed spec: {}", detail),
162:             Self::MissingStep { step_id } => write!(f, "missing step: {}", step_id),
163:             Self::InvalidDependency {
164:                 step_id,
165:                 missing_dep,
166:             } => {
167:                 write!(
168:                     f,
169:                     "step {} depends on missing step {}",
170:                     step_id, missing_dep
171:                 )
172:             }
173:             Self::UnsupportedVersion { version } => {
174:                 write!(f, "unsupported spec version: {}", version)
175:             }
176:             Self::MissingPrimitive { step_id } => {
177:                 write!(f, "missing primitive on step: {}", step_id)
178:             }
179:         }
180:     }
181: }
182: 
183: impl fmt::Display for ValidationError {
184:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
185:         match self {
186:             Self::HashMismatch {
187:                 spec_id,
188:                 calculated,
189:             } => {
190:                 write!(
191:                     f,
192:                     "hash mismatch: spec_id={}, calculated={}",
193:                     spec_id, calculated
194:                 )
195:             }
196:             Self::DependencyViolation {
197:                 step_id,
198:                 parent_id,
199:                 detail,
200:             } => {
201:                 write!(
202:                     f,
203:                     "dependency violation: {} -> {}: {}",
204:                     step_id, parent_id, detail
205:                 )
206:             }
207:             Self::CapabilityMismatch {
208:                 step_id,
209:                 worker_id,
210:                 required,
211:                 actual,
212:             } => {
213:                 write!(
214:                     f,
215:                     "capability mismatch on step {}: worker {} has {}, requires {}",
216:                     step_id, worker_id, actual, required
217:                 )
218:             }
219:             Self::UnknownEventType { event_type } => {
220:                 write!(f, "unknown event type: {}", event_type)
221:             }
222:             Self::InvalidTransition { from, to, reason } => {
223:                 write!(f, "invalid transition {} -> {}: {}", from, to, reason)
224:             }
225:         }
226:     }
227: }
228: 
229: impl fmt::Display for ExecutionError {
230:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
231:         match self {
232:             Self::PrimitiveStartFailed { step_id, reason } => {
233:                 write!(f, "primitive start failed for {}: {}", step_id, reason)
234:             }
235:             Self::PrimitiveTimeout {
236:                 step_id,
237:                 timeout_secs,
238:             } => {
239:                 write!(f, "primitive {} timed out after {}s", step_id, timeout_secs)
240:             }
241:             Self::LeaseExpired { step_id, lease_id } => {
242:                 write!(f, "lease {} expired for step {}", lease_id, step_id)
243:             }
244:             Self::DoubleCommit { step_id } => write!(f, "double commit on step {}", step_id),
245:             Self::StaleClaim { step_id, worker_id } => {
246:                 write!(f, "stale claim by {} on step {}", worker_id, step_id)
247:             }
248:         }
249:     }
250: }
251: 
252: impl fmt::Display for ProviderError {
253:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
254:         match self {
255:             Self::Filesystem {
256:                 operation,
257:                 path,
258:                 detail,
259:             } => {
260:                 write!(f, "filesystem {}: {} — {}", operation, path, detail)
261:             }
262:             Self::Llm {
263:                 prompt_hash,
264:                 detail,
265:             } => {
266:                 write!(f, "llm (prompt {}): {}", prompt_hash, detail)
267:             }
268:             Self::Persistence { operation, detail } => {
269:                 write!(f, "persistence {}: {}", operation, detail)
270:             }
271:             Self::NotRegistered { provider_type } => {
272:                 write!(f, "provider not registered: {}", provider_type)
273:             }
274:         }
275:     }
276: }
277: 
278: impl fmt::Display for ReconstructionError {
279:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
280:         match self {
281:             Self::CorruptedEventLog { task_id, detail } => {
282:                 write!(f, "corrupted event log for task {}: {}", task_id, detail)
283:             }
284:             Self::ReplayDivergence { task_id, detail } => {
285:                 write!(f, "replay divergence for task {}: {}", task_id, detail)
286:             }
287:             Self::OrphanedEffects { task_id, count } => {
288:                 write!(f, "task {} has {} orphaned effects", task_id, count)
289:             }
290:             Self::CausalOrderViolation { unit_id, detail } => {
291:                 write!(f, "causal order violation in unit {}: {}", unit_id, detail)
292:             }
293:         }
294:     }
295: }

173:             "SELECT exec_spec, task_class FROM tasks WHERE task_id = ?1",
174:             [task_id],
175:             |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)),
176:         )
177:         .optional()?;
178: 
179:     let (exec_spec_opt, task_class_str) =
180:         row.ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("missing task record for task_id '{}'", task_id))?;
181: 
182:     let spec = if let Some(json_str) = exec_spec_opt {
183:         if !json_str.trim().is_empty() {
184:             serde_json::from_str::<ExecSpec>(&json_str)?
185:         } else {
186:             let task_class = match task_class_str.as_str() {
187:                 "Generic" => TaskClass::Generic,
188:                 "PlannerHardening" => TaskClass::PlannerHardening,
189:                 "CodeFix" => TaskClass::CodeFix,
190:                 other => anyhow::bail!("unknown task_class '{}'", other),
191:             };
192:             task_class.to_exec_spec(None)
193:         }
194:     } else {
195:         let task_class = match task_class_str.as_str() {
196:             "Generic" => TaskClass::Generic,
197:             "PlannerHardening" => TaskClass::PlannerHardening,
198:             "CodeFix" => TaskClass::CodeFix,
199:             other => anyhow::bail!("unknown task_class '{}'", other),
200:         };
201:         task_class.to_exec_spec(None)
202:     };
203: 
204:     Ok(spec.steps.iter().map(|s| s.step_id.clone()).collect())
205: }
206: 
207: fn status_outcome(status: &str) -> Option<StepOutcome> {
208:     match status {
209:         "committed" => Some(StepOutcome::Success),
210:         "rejected" => Some(StepOutcome::TerminalFailure),
211:         _ => None,
212:     }
213: }
214: 
215: fn terminal_outcome(outcome: &StepOutcome) -> bool {
216:     matches!(outcome, StepOutcome::Success | StepOutcome::TerminalFailure)
217: }
218: 
219: fn failed_step_status_from_payload(payload: &str) -> &'static str {
220:     let outcome = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(payload)
221:         .ok()
222:         .and_then(|v| {
223:             v.get("outcome").map(|o| match o.as_str() {
224:                 Some("RetryableFailure") => StepOutcome::RetryableFailure,
225:                 Some("Blocked") => StepOutcome::Blocked,
226:                 _ => StepOutcome::TerminalFailure,
227:             })
228:         })
229:         .unwrap_or(StepOutcome::TerminalFailure);
230: 
231:     match outcome {
232:         StepOutcome::RetryableFailure | StepOutcome::Blocked => "pending",
233:         _ => "rejected",
234:     }
235: }
236: 
237: fn replay_events(conn: &Connection, task_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
238:     let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
239:         "SELECT step_id, event_type, payload
240:          FROM event_log
241:          WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id IS NOT NULL
242:          ORDER BY causal_unit_id, sequence_in_unit, id",
243:     )?;
244: 
245:     let rows = stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
246:         Ok((
247:             r.get::<_, String>(0)?,
248:             r.get::<_, String>(1)?,
249:             r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
250:         ))
251:     })?;
252: 
253:     for row in rows.filter_map(|r| r.ok()) {
254:         let (step_id, event_type, payload) = row;
255: 
256:         match event_type.as_str() {
257:             "STEP_COMPLETED" | "PrimitiveCompleted" => {
258:                 conn.execute(
259:                     "UPDATE step_status SET status = 'committed'
260:                      WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
261:                     params![task_id, step_id],
262:                 )?;
263:                 conn.execute(
264:                     "UPDATE leases
265:                      SET state = 'completed'
266:                      WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND state = 'active'",
267:                     params![task_id, step_id],
268:                 )?;
269:             }
270:             "STEP_FAILED" | "PrimitiveFailed" => {
271:                 conn.execute(
272:                     "UPDATE step_status SET status = ?3
273:                      WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
274:                     params![task_id, step_id, failed_step_status_from_payload(&payload)],
275:                 )?;
276:                 conn.execute(
277:                     "UPDATE leases
278:                      SET state = 'released'
279:                      WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND state = 'active'",
280:                     params![task_id, step_id],
281:                 )?;
282:             }
283:             "STEP_DISPATCHED" | "PrimitiveScheduled" => {
284:                 let current: String = conn.query_row(
285:                     "SELECT status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
286:                     params![task_id, step_id],
287:                     |r| r.get(0),
288:                 )?;
289:                 if !status_outcome(&current)
290:                     .as_ref()
291:                     .map(terminal_outcome)
292:                     .unwrap_or(false)
293:                 {
294:                     conn.execute(
295:                         "UPDATE step_status SET status = 'dispatched'
296:                          WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
297:                         params![task_id, step_id],
298:                     )?;
299:                 }
300:             }
301:             "LEASE_EXPIRED" => {
302:                 let current: String = conn.query_row(
303:                     "SELECT status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
304:                     params![task_id, step_id],
305:                     |r| r.get(0),
306:                 )?;
307:                 if !status_outcome(&current)
308:                     .as_ref()
309:                     .map(terminal_outcome)
310:                     .unwrap_or(false)
311:                 {
312:                     conn.execute(
313:                         "UPDATE step_status SET status = 'pending'
314:                          WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
315:                         params![task_id, step_id],
316:                     )?;
317:                     conn.execute(
318:                         "UPDATE leases
319:                          SET state = 'expired'
320:                          WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND state = 'active'",
321:                         params![task_id, step_id],
322:                     )?;
323:                 }
324:             }
325:             _ => {}
326:         }
327:     }
328: 
329:     Ok(())
330: }
331: 
332: fn unlock_ready_steps(conn: &Connection, task_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
333:     let all_steps = ordered_step_ids(conn, task_id)?;
334: 
335:     for step_id in &all_steps {
336:         let current: String = conn.query_row(
337:             "SELECT status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
338:             params![task_id, step_id],
339:             |r| r.get(0),
340:         )?;
341: 
342:         if status_outcome(&current)
343:             .as_ref()
344:             .map(terminal_outcome)
345:             .unwrap_or(false)
346:             || current == "dispatched"
347:             || current == "ready"
348:         {
349:             continue;
350:         }
351: 
352:         let dep_total: i64 = conn.query_row(
353:             "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM step_dependencies WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
354:             params![task_id, step_id],
355:             |r| r.get(0),
356:         )?;
357: 
358:         if dep_total == 0 {
359:             conn.execute(
360:                 "UPDATE step_status SET status = 'ready'
361:                  WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND status = 'pending'",
362:                 params![task_id, step_id],
363:             )?;
364:             continue;
365:         }
366: 
367:         let dep_satisfied: i64 = conn.query_row(
368:             "SELECT COUNT(*)
369:              FROM step_dependencies d
370:              JOIN step_status s
371:                ON s.task_id = d.task_id
372:               AND s.step_id = d.depends_on_step_id
373:              WHERE d.task_id = ?1
374:                AND d.step_id = ?2
375:                AND s.status = 'committed'",
376:             params![task_id, step_id],
377:             |r| r.get(0),
378:         )?;
379: 
380:         if dep_satisfied == dep_total {
381:             conn.execute(
382:                 "UPDATE step_status SET status = 'ready'
383:                  WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND status = 'pending'",
384:                 params![task_id, step_id],
385:             )?;
386:         }
387:     }
388: 
389:     Ok(())
390: }
391: 
392: fn classify_failure_outcome(reason: &str) -> StepOutcome {
393:     if reason.starts_with("retry:") || reason.contains("timeout waiting for lock") {
394:         return StepOutcome::RetryableFailure;
395:     }
396:     if reason.starts_with("blocked:") {
397:         return StepOutcome::Blocked;
398:     }
399:     StepOutcome::TerminalFailure
400: }
401: 
402: fn outcome_to_event_type(outcome: StepOutcome) -> &'static str {
403:     match outcome {
404:         StepOutcome::Success => "STEP_COMPLETED",
405:         StepOutcome::TerminalFailure => "STEP_FAILED",
406:         StepOutcome::RetryableFailure | StepOutcome::Blocked => "STEP_FAILED",
407:     }
408: }
409: 
410: fn is_ai_worker(worker_id: &str) -> bool {
411:     let lower = worker_id.to_ascii_lowercase();
412:     lower == "ai"
413:         || lower.contains("worker-ai")
414:         || lower.contains("ai-worker")
415:         || lower.starts_with("ai-")
416:         || lower.starts_with("ai_")
417: }
418: 
419: fn capability_for_worker_id(worker_id: &str) -> Result<WorkerCapability> {
420:     let lower = worker_id.to_ascii_lowercase();
421: 
422:     if is_ai_worker(worker_id) {
423:         return Ok(WorkerCapability::LegacyGeneric);
424:     }
425: 
426:     if lower.contains("planner") {
427:         return Ok(WorkerCapability::Planner);
428:     }
429: 
430:     if lower.contains("executor") {
431:         return Ok(WorkerCapability::Executor);
432:     }
433: 
434:     if lower.contains("verifier") {
435:         return Ok(WorkerCapability::Verifier);
436:     }
437: 
438:     if lower.starts_with("worker-") || lower.starts_with("worker_") {
439:         return Ok(WorkerCapability::LegacyGeneric);
440:     }
441: 
442:     Err(anyhow!("worker has no declared capability: {}", worker_id))
443: }
444: 
445: fn canonical_json(value: &Value) -> Result<String> {
446:     let mut ordered = BTreeMap::new();
447:     if let Value::Object(map) = value {
448:         for (k, v) in map {
449:             ordered.insert(k.clone(), v.clone());
450:         }
451:         Ok(serde_json::to_string(&ordered)?)
452:     } else {
453:         Ok(serde_json::to_string(value)?)
454:     }
455: }

457: // StorageProvider Implementation
458: impl StorageProvider for DefaultStorage {
459:     fn get_latest_snapshot_payload(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {
460:         let conn = self.conn()?;
461:         let payload: Option<String> = conn
462:             .query_row(
463:                 "SELECT payload FROM state_snapshots WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY snapshot_id DESC LIMIT 1",
464:                 [task_id],
465:                 |r| r.get(0),
466:             )
467:             .optional()?;
468:         Ok(payload)
469:     }
470: 
471:     fn load_exec_spec(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<ExecSpec> {
472:         let conn = self.conn()?;
473:         let row: Option<(Option<String>, String)> = conn
474:             .query_row(
475:                 "SELECT exec_spec, task_class FROM tasks WHERE task_id = ?1",
476:                 [task_id],
477:                 |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)),
478:             )
479:             .optional()?;
480: 
481:         let (exec_spec_opt, task_class_str) =
482:             row.ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("missing task record for task_id '{}'", task_id))?;
483: 
484:         if let Some(json_str) = exec_spec_opt {
485:             if !json_str.trim().is_empty() {
486:                 if let Ok(spec) = serde_json::from_str::<ExecSpec>(&json_str) {
487:                     return Ok(spec);
488:                 }
489:             }
490:         }
491: 
492:         let task_class = match task_class_str.as_str() {
493:             "Generic" => TaskClass::Generic,
494:             "PlannerHardening" => TaskClass::PlannerHardening,
495:             "CodeFix" => TaskClass::CodeFix,
496:             other => anyhow::bail!("unknown task_class '{}'", other),
497:         };
498:         Ok(task_class.to_exec_spec(None))
499:     }
500: 
501:     fn update_step_status(&self, task_id: &str, step_id: &str, status: &str) -> Result<()> {
502:         let conn = self.conn()?;
503:         conn.execute(
504:             "UPDATE step_status SET status = ?3 WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
505:             params![task_id, step_id, status],
506:         )?;
507:         Ok(())
508:     }
509: 
510:     fn get_step_status(&self, task_id: &str, step_id: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {
511:         let conn = self.conn()?;
512:         let status: Option<String> = conn
513:             .query_row(
514:                 "SELECT status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
515:                 [task_id, step_id],
516:                 |r| r.get(0),
517:             )
518:             .optional()?;
519:         Ok(status)
520:     }
521: 
522:     fn append_event(
523:         &self,
524:         task_id: &str,
525:         step_id: Option<&str>,
526:         event_type: &str,
527:         payload: &Value,
528:     ) -> Result<i64> {
529:         let mut conn = self.conn()?;
530:         let tx = conn.transaction()?;
531: 
532:         let unit_gen: i64 = tx.query_row(
533:             "INSERT INTO generations DEFAULT VALUES RETURNING id",
534:             [],
535:             |r| r.get(0),
536:         )?;
537: 
538:         let event_id = payload
539:             .get("event_id")
540:             .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
541:             .map(|s| s.to_string())
542:             .unwrap_or_else(|| {
543:                 format!(
544:                     "evt_{}",
545:                     &blake3::hash(format!("{}:{}:{}", task_id, event_type, unit_gen).as_bytes())
546:                         .to_hex()[..16]
547:                 )
548:             });
549: 
550:         let payload_str = canonical_json(payload)?;
551: 
552:         tx.execute(
553:             "INSERT INTO event_log
554:              (event_id, system_generation, causal_unit_id, sequence_in_unit, task_id, step_id, event_type, payload, logical_generation)
555:              VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 0, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)",
556:             params![
557:                 event_id,
558:                 unit_gen,
559:                 unit_gen,
560:                 task_id,
561:                 step_id,
562:                 event_type,
563:                 payload_str,
564:                 unit_gen
565:             ],
566:         )?;
567: 
568:         tx.commit()?;
569:         Ok(unit_gen)
570:     }
571: 
572:     fn append_semantic_artifact(
573:         &self,
574:         task_id: &str,
575:         step_id: &str,
576:         generation: i64,
577:         artifact_type: &str,
578:         payload: &Value,
579:     ) -> Result<()> {
580:         let conn = self.conn()?;
581:         let max_gen: i64 = conn.query_row(
582:             "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(source_generation), -1) FROM semantic_artifacts WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
583:             params![task_id, step_id],
584:             |r| r.get(0),
585:         )?;
586:         if generation < max_gen {
587:             return Err(anyhow!(
588:                 "generation fence: source_generation {} <= existing max {}",
589:                 generation,
590:                 max_gen
591:             ));
592:         }
593:         let payload_str = canonical_json(payload)?;
594:         conn.execute(
595:             "INSERT INTO semantic_artifacts
596:              (task_id, step_id, source_generation, artifact_type, payload)
597:              VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5)",
598:             params![task_id, step_id, generation, artifact_type, payload_str],
599:         )?;
600:         Ok(())
601:     }
602: 
603:     fn latest_generation_for_task(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<i64> {
604:         let conn = self.conn()?;
605:         let generation = conn.query_row(
606:             "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(system_generation), 0) FROM event_log WHERE task_id = ?1",
607:             [task_id],
608:             |r| r.get(0),
609:         )?;
610:         Ok(generation)
611:     }
612: 
613:     fn claim_worker(&self, task_id: &str, worker_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
614:         let mut conn = self.conn()?;
615:         let tx = conn.transaction()?;
616: 
617:         let row: Option<(String, String)> = tx
618:             .query_row(
619:                 "SELECT l.lease_id, l.step_id
620:                  FROM leases l
621:                  JOIN step_status s
622:                    ON s.task_id = l.task_id
623:                   AND s.step_id = l.step_id
624:                  WHERE l.task_id = ?1
625:                    AND l.state = 'active'
626:                    AND s.status = 'dispatched'
627:                    AND l.worker_id = 'worker-scheduler'
628:                  ORDER BY l.acquired_generation, l.step_id
629:                  LIMIT 1",
630:                 [task_id],
631:                 |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)),
632:             )
633:             .optional()?;
634: 
635:         let (lease_id, step_id) =
636:             row.ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("no dispatchable active lease found"))?;
637: 
638:         let current_owner: String = tx.query_row(
639:             "SELECT worker_id FROM leases WHERE lease_id = ?1 AND state = 'active'",
640:             [lease_id.clone()],
641:             |r| r.get(0),
642:         )?;
643: 
644:         if current_owner == worker_id {
645:             tx.commit()?;
646:             return Ok(());
647:         }
648: 
649:         if current_owner != "worker-scheduler" {
650:             return Err(anyhow!("lease already owned by {}", current_owner));
651:         }
652: 
653:         let updated = tx.execute(
654:             "UPDATE leases
655:              SET worker_id = ?1
656:              WHERE lease_id = ?2
657:                AND state = 'active'
658:                AND worker_id = 'worker-scheduler'",
659:             params![worker_id, lease_id],
660:         )?;
661: 
662:         if updated == 0 {
663:             return Err(anyhow!("lease claim lost"));
664:         }
665: 
666:         let next_generation: i64 = tx.query_row(
667:             "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(system_generation), 0) + 1 FROM event_log",
668:             [],
669:             |r| r.get(0),
670:         )?;
671: 
672:         let claim_payload = json!({
673:             "lease_id": lease_id,
674:             "worker_id": worker_id
675:         });
676:         let claim_payload_str = canonical_json(&claim_payload)?;
677: 
678:         tx.execute(
679:             "INSERT INTO event_log
680:              (system_generation, causal_unit_id, sequence_in_unit, task_id, step_id, event_type, payload, logical_generation)
681:              VALUES (?1, ?2, 0, ?3, ?4, 'WORKER_CLAIMED', ?5, ?6)",
682:             params![
683:                 next_generation,
684:                 next_generation,
685:                 task_id,
686:                 step_id,
687:                 claim_payload_str,
688:                 next_generation
689:             ],
690:         )?;
691: 
692:         tx.commit()?;
693:         println!("WORKER_CLAIM_OK");
694:         println!("WORKER: {}", worker_id);
695:         println!("STEP_CLAIMED: {}", step_id);
696:         Ok(())
697:     }
698: 
699:     fn get_active_lease(
700:         &self,
701:         task_id: &str,
702:         step_id: &str,
703:         worker_id: &str,
704:     ) -> Result<Option<String>> {
705:         let conn = self.conn()?;
706:         let lease_id: Option<String> = conn
707:             .query_row(
708:                 "SELECT lease_id
709:                  FROM leases
710:                  WHERE task_id = ?1
711:                    AND step_id = ?2
712:                    AND worker_id = ?3
713:                    AND state = 'active'
714:                  ORDER BY acquired_generation DESC
715:                  LIMIT 1",
716:                 params![task_id, step_id, worker_id],
717:                 |r| r.get(0),
718:             )
719:             .optional()?;
720:         Ok(lease_id)
721:     }

725:         conn.execute(
726:             "UPDATE leases SET state = ?2 WHERE lease_id = ?1",
727:             params![lease_id, state],
728:         )?;
729:         Ok(())
730:     }

741:              SET expires_at_generation = ?3
742:              WHERE lease_id = ?1
743:                AND worker_id = ?2

746:         )?;

750:     fn find_dispatched_step(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {
751:         let conn = self.conn()?;
752:         let step_id: Option<String> = conn
753:             .query_row(
754:                 "SELECT step_id
755:                  FROM step_status
756:                  WHERE task_id = ?1 AND status = 'dispatched'
757:                  ORDER BY step_id
758:                  LIMIT 1",
759:                 [task_id],
760:                 |r| r.get(0),
761:             )
762:             .optional()?;
763:         Ok(step_id)
764:     }
765: 
766:     fn process_effects_ledger(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
767:         let mut conn = self.conn()?;
768:         let tx = conn.transaction()?;
769: 
770:         let mut reserve_stmt = tx.prepare(
771:             "SELECT step_id, payload
772:              FROM event_log
773:              WHERE task_id = ?1 AND event_type IN ('EFFECT_RESERVED', 'ArtifactProduced')
774:              ORDER BY id",
775:         )?;
776:         let reserve_rows = reserve_stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
777:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
778:         })?;
779:         let mut reserved = Vec::new();
780:         for r in reserve_rows {
781:             reserved.push(r?);
782:         }
783:         drop(reserve_stmt);
784: 
785:         for (step_id, payload) in reserved {
786:             let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&payload)?;
787:             if let Some(effect_id) = v.get("effect_id").and_then(|x| x.as_str()) {
788:                 tx.execute(
789:                     "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO effect_ledger
790:                      (effect_id, task_id, step_id, reservation_generation, state)
791:                      VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 0, 'reserved')",
792:                     params![effect_id, task_id, step_id],
793:                 )?;
794:             }
795:         }
796: 
797:         let mut complete_stmt = tx.prepare(
798:             "SELECT step_id, payload
799:              FROM event_log
800:              WHERE task_id = ?1 AND event_type IN ('STEP_COMPLETED', 'PrimitiveCompleted')
801:              ORDER BY id",
802:         )?;
803:         let complete_rows = complete_stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
804:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
805:         })?;
806:         let mut completed = Vec::new();
807:         for r in complete_rows {
808:             completed.push(r?);
809:         }
810:         drop(complete_stmt);
811: 
812:         for (step_id, payload) in completed {
813:             let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&payload)?;
814:             if let Some(effect_id) = v.get("effect_id").and_then(|x| x.as_str()) {
815:                 tx.execute(
816:                     "UPDATE effect_ledger
817:                      SET state = 'committed'
818:                      WHERE effect_id = ?1 AND task_id = ?2 AND step_id = ?3",
819:                     params![effect_id, task_id, step_id],
820:                 )?;
821:             }
822:         }
823: 
824:         let mut fail_stmt = tx.prepare(
825:             "SELECT step_id, payload
826:              FROM event_log
827:              WHERE task_id = ?1 AND event_type IN ('STEP_FAILED', 'PrimitiveFailed')
828:              ORDER BY id",
829:         )?;
830:         let fail_rows = fail_stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
831:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
832:         })?;
833:         let mut failed = Vec::new();
834:         for r in fail_rows {
835:             failed.push(r?);
836:         }
837:         drop(fail_stmt);
838: 
839:         for (step_id, payload) in failed {
840:             let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&payload)?;
841:             if let Some(effect_id) = v.get("effect_id").and_then(|x| x.as_str()) {
842:                 tx.execute(
843:                     "UPDATE effect_ledger
844:                      SET state = 'rejected'
845:                      WHERE effect_id = ?1 AND task_id = ?2 AND step_id = ?3",
846:                     params![effect_id, task_id, step_id],
847:                 )?;
848:             }
849:         }
850: 
851:         let mut ext_stmt = tx.prepare(
852:             "SELECT e.effect_id, e.task_id, e.step_id, e.state
853:              FROM effect_ledger e
854:              LEFT JOIN external_effects x ON x.effect_id = e.effect_id
855:              WHERE e.task_id = ?1
856:                AND e.state IN ('committed','rejected')
857:                AND x.effect_id IS NULL
858:              ORDER BY e.effect_id",
859:         )?;
860:         let rows = ext_stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
861:             Ok((
862:                 r.get::<_, String>(0)?,
863:                 r.get::<_, String>(1)?,
864:                 r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
865:                 r.get::<_, String>(3)?,
866:             ))
867:         })?;
868:         let mut unprocessed = Vec::new();
869:         for r in rows {
870:             unprocessed.push(r?);
871:         }
872:         drop(ext_stmt);
873: 
874:         let mut count = 0_i64;
875:         for (effect_id, task_id, step_id, observed_state) in unprocessed {
876:             let result_payload = if observed_state == "committed" {
877:                 serde_json::json!({
878:                     "effect_id": effect_id,
879:                     "action": "send_to_external_system",
880:                     "status": "executed"
881:                 })
882:             } else {
883:                 serde_json::json!({
884:                     "effect_id": effect_id,
885:                     "action": "skip_external_side_effect",
886:                     "status": "suppressed_due_to_rejection"
887:                 })
888:             };
889:             let result_payload_str = canonical_json(&result_payload)?;
890: 
891:             tx.execute(
892:                 "INSERT INTO external_effects
893:                  (effect_id, task_id, step_id, observed_state, result_payload)
894:                  VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5)",
895:                 params![
896:                     effect_id,
897:                     task_id,
898:                     step_id,
899:                     observed_state,
900:                     result_payload_str
901:                 ],
902:             )?;
903:             count += 1;
904:         }
905: 
906:         tx.commit()?;
907:         println!("EFFECT_EXECUTION_OK");
908:         println!("EXECUTED_EFFECT_ROWS: {}", count);
909:         Ok(())
910:     }
911: 
912:     fn rebuild_snapshot(&self, task_id: &str, quiet: bool) -> Result<()> {
913:         let conn = self.conn()?;
914: 
915:         let latest = conn.query_row(
916:             "SELECT payload FROM state_snapshots WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY snapshot_id DESC LIMIT 1",
917:             [task_id],
918:             |r| r.get::<_, String>(0),
919:         ).optional()?;
920: 
921:         let (base_generation, mut steps, mut done) = match latest {
922:             Some(payload_str) => {
923:                 let payload: Value = serde_json::from_str(&payload_str)?;
924:                 let last_generation = payload
925:                     .get("last_generation")
926:                     .and_then(|v| v.as_i64())
927:                     .unwrap_or(0);
928:                 let done = payload
929:                     .get("done")
930:                     .and_then(|v| v.as_bool())
931:                     .unwrap_or(false);
932: 
933:                 let mut steps = BTreeMap::new();
934:                 if let Some(obj) = payload.get("steps").and_then(|v| v.as_object()) {
935:                     for (k, v) in obj {
936:                         if let Some(s) = v.as_str() {
937:                             steps.insert(k.clone(), s.to_string());
938:                         }
939:                     }
940:                 }
941:                 (last_generation, steps, done)
942:             }
943:             None => (0, BTreeMap::new(), false),
944:         };
945: 
946:         let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
947:             "SELECT system_generation, step_id, event_type, payload
948:              FROM event_log
949:              WHERE task_id = ?1 AND system_generation > ?2
950:              ORDER BY causal_unit_id, sequence_in_unit",
951:         )?;
952: 
953:         let rows = stmt.query_map(params![task_id, base_generation], |r| {
954:             Ok((
955:                 r.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
956:                 r.get::<_, Option<String>>(1)?,
957:                 r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
958:                 r.get::<_, String>(3)?,
959:             ))
960:         })?;
961: 
962:         let mut last_generation = base_generation;
963:         for row in rows {
964:             let (generation, step_id, event_type, _payload) = row?;
965:             last_generation = generation;
966: 
967:             match event_type.as_str() {
968:                 "STEP_COMPLETED" | "PrimitiveCompleted" => {
969:                     if let Some(step_id) = step_id {
970:                         steps.insert(step_id, "committed".to_string());
971:                     }
972:                 }
973:                 "STEP_FAILED" | "PrimitiveFailed" => {
974:                     if let Some(step_id) = step_id {
975:                         steps.insert(step_id, "rejected".to_string());
976:                     }
977:                 }
978:                 "STEP_DISPATCHED" | "PrimitiveScheduled" => {
979:                     if let Some(step_id) = step_id {
980:                         steps
981:                             .entry(step_id)
982:                             .or_insert_with(|| "dispatched".to_string());
983:                     }
984:                 }
985:                 "LEASE_ACQUIRED" => {
986:                     if let Some(step_id) = step_id {
987:                         steps.entry(step_id).or_insert_with(|| "leased".to_string());
988:                     }
989:                 }
990:                 "DONE" => {
991:                     done = true;
992:                 }
993:                 _ => {}
994:             }
995:         }
996: 
997:         let mut artifact_refs: BTreeMap<String, Value> = BTreeMap::new();
998:         let mut stmt2 = conn.prepare(
999:             "SELECT artifact_type, artifact_id FROM semantic_artifacts WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY artifact_id DESC",
1000:         )?;
1001:         let rows2 = stmt2.query_map([task_id], |r| {
1002:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, i64>(1)?))
1003:         })?;
1004:         for row in rows2 {
1005:             let (artifact_type, artifact_id) = row?;
1006:             artifact_refs
1007:                 .entry(artifact_type)
1008:                 .or_insert_with(|| json!(artifact_id));
1009:         }
1010: 
1011:         let state_payload = json!({
1012:             "task_id": task_id,
1013:             "last_generation": last_generation,
1014:             "done": done,
1015:             "steps": steps,
1016:             "artifacts": artifact_refs
1017:         });
1018: 
1019:         let created_at = last_generation;
1020:         let state_hash = serde_json::to_string(&state_payload)?.len() as u64;
1021: 
1022:         let payload = json!({
1023:             "snapshot_version": 1,
1024:             "schema_version": 1,
1025:             "created_at": created_at,
1026:             "state_hash": state_hash,
1027:             "state": state_payload,
1028:             "task_id": task_id,
1029:             "last_generation": last_generation,
1030:             "done": done,
1031:             "steps": steps,
1032:             "artifacts": artifact_refs
1033:         });
1034: 
1035:         conn.execute(
1036:             "INSERT INTO state_snapshots (task_id, last_generation, payload) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
1037:             params![task_id, last_generation, serde_json::to_string(&payload)?],
1038:         )?;
1039: 
1040:         if !quiet {
1041:             println!("SNAPSHOT OK");
1042:             println!("SNAPSHOT_BASE_GENERATION: {}", base_generation);
1043:             println!("SNAPSHOT_GENERATION: {}", last_generation);
1044:         }
1045:         Ok(())
1046:     }
1047: 
1048:     fn restore_snapshot(&self, task_id: &str, quiet: bool) -> Result<()> {
1049:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1050: 
1051:         let (snapshot_id, last_generation, payload): (i64, i64, String) = conn.query_row(
1052:             "SELECT snapshot_id, last_generation, payload FROM state_snapshots WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY snapshot_id DESC LIMIT 1",
1053:             [task_id],
1054:             |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?, r.get(2)?)),
1055:         )?;
1056: 
1057:         let payload_json: Value = serde_json::from_str(&payload)?;
1058:         let snapshot_version = payload_json
1059:             .get("snapshot_version")
1060:             .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
1061:             .unwrap_or(1);
1062:         let schema_version = payload_json
1063:             .get("schema_version")
1064:             .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
1065:             .unwrap_or(1);
1066: 
1067:         if snapshot_version > 1 {
1068:             return Err(anyhow!(
1069:                 "unsupported future snapshot_version: {}",
1070:                 snapshot_version
1071:             ));
1072:         }
1073:         if schema_version > 1 {
1074:             return Err(anyhow!(
1075:                 "unsupported future schema_version: {}",
1076:                 schema_version
1077:             ));
1078:         }
1079: 
1080:         if !quiet {
1081:             println!("RESTORE OK");
1082:             println!("SNAPSHOT_ID: {}", snapshot_id);
1083:             println!("SNAPSHOT_GENERATION: {}", last_generation);
1084: 
1085:             if let Some(artifacts) = payload_json.get("artifacts").and_then(|v| v.as_object()) {
1086:                 for (artifact_type, artifact_id) in artifacts {
1087:                     println!("ARTIFACT_REF\t{}\t{}", artifact_type, artifact_id);
1088:                 }
1089:             }
1090:             println!("{}", payload);
1091:         }
1092:         Ok(())
1093:     }
1094: 
1095:     fn set_override_path(&self, path: Option<String>) {
1096:         OVERRIDE_PATH.with(|p| {
1097:             *p.borrow_mut() = path;
1098:         });
1099:     }

1101:     fn list_event_log(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<EventLogRows> {
1102:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1103:         let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
1104:             "SELECT system_generation, step_id, event_type, payload
1105:              FROM event_log
1106:              WHERE task_id = ?1
1107:              ORDER BY system_generation, id",
1108:         )?;
1109: 
1110:         let rows = stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
1111:             Ok((
1112:                 r.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
1113:                 r.get::<_, Option<String>>(1)?,
1114:                 r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
1115:                 r.get::<_, String>(3)?,
1116:             ))
1117:         })?;
1118: 
1119:         let mut res = Vec::new();
1120:         for r in rows {
1121:             res.push(r?);
1122:         }
1123:         Ok(res)
1124:     }

1126:     fn seed_dependencies(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
1127:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1128:         let ordered = ordered_step_ids(&conn, task_id)?;
1129: 
1130:         for window in ordered.windows(2) {
1131:             let dep = &window[0];
1132:             let step = &window[1];
1133:             conn.execute(
1134:                 "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO step_dependencies (task_id, step_id, depends_on_step_id)
1135:                  VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
1136:                 params![task_id, step, dep],
1137:             )?;
1138:         }
1139: 
1140:         for step in ordered {
1141:             conn.execute(
1142:                 "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO step_status (task_id, step_id, status)
1143:                  VALUES (?1, ?2, 'pending')",
1144:                 params![task_id, step],
1145:             )?;
1146:         }
1147: 
1148:         Ok(())
1149:     }
1150: 
1151:     fn reconcile_scheduler(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
1152:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1153:         self.seed_dependencies(task_id)?;
1154: 
1155:         conn.execute(
1156:             "UPDATE step_status SET status = 'pending' WHERE task_id = ?1",
1157:             [task_id],
1158:         )?;
1159: 
1160:         replay_events(&conn, task_id)?;
1161:         unlock_ready_steps(&conn, task_id)?;
1162: 
1163:         let mut status: BTreeMap<String, String> = BTreeMap::new();
1164:         let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
1165:             "SELECT step_id, status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY step_id",
1166:         )?;
1167:         let rows = stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
1168:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
1169:         })?;
1170: 
1171:         for row in rows.filter_map(|r| r.ok()) {
1172:             status.insert(row.0, row.1);
1173:         }
1174: 
1175:         println!("RECONCILE OK");
1176:         println!("STEP_STATUS: {:?}", status);
1177:         Ok(status)
1178:     }
1179: 
1180:     fn schedule_next_steps(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
1181:         let mut conn = self.conn()?;
1182:         self.seed_dependencies(task_id)?;
1183:         let tx = conn.transaction()?;
1184: 
1185:         tx.execute(
1186:             "UPDATE step_status
1187:              SET status = 'pending'
1188:              WHERE task_id = ?1
1189:                AND status NOT IN ('dispatched','committed','rejected')",
1190:             [task_id],
1191:         )?;
1192: 
1193:         tx.execute(
1194:             "UPDATE step_status
1195:              SET status = 'ready'
1196:              WHERE task_id = ?1
1197:                AND status = 'dispatched'
1198:                AND NOT EXISTS (
1199:                    SELECT 1 FROM leases
1200:                    WHERE task_id = ?1
1201:                      AND step_id = step_status.step_id
1202:                      AND state = 'active'
1203:                )",
1204:             [task_id],
1205:         )?;
1206: 
1207:         replay_events(&tx, task_id)?;
1208:         unlock_ready_steps(&tx, task_id)?;
1209: 
1210:         let ready: Vec<String> = ordered_step_ids(&tx, task_id)?
1211:             .into_iter()
1212:             .filter(|step_id| {
1213:                 let status: Option<String> = tx
1214:                     .query_row(
1215:                         "SELECT status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
1216:                         params![task_id, step_id],
1217:                         |r| r.get(0),
1218:                     )
1219:                     .optional()
1220:                     .unwrap_or(None);
1221: 
1222:                 if !matches!(status.as_deref(), Some("ready") | Some("dispatched")) {
1223:                     return false;
1224:                 }
1225: 
1226:                 let active_lease: Option<i64> = tx
1227:                     .query_row(
1228:                         "SELECT 1 FROM leases WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND state = 'active' LIMIT 1",
1229:                         params![task_id, step_id],
1230:                         |r| r.get(0),
1231:                     )
1232:                     .optional()
1233:                     .unwrap_or(None);
1234: 
1235:                 active_lease.is_none()
1236:             })
1237:             .collect();
1238:         println!("READY_QUEUE: {:?}", ready);
1239: 
1240:         for step_id in ready {
1241:             let current_generation: i64 = tx.query_row(
1242:                 "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(system_generation), 0) FROM event_log",
1243:                 [],
1244:                 |r| r.get(0),
1245:             )?;
1246: 
1247:             let lease_seq: i64 = tx.query_row(
1248:                 "SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*), 0) + 1 FROM leases WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
1249:                 params![task_id, step_id],
1250:                 |r| r.get(0),
1251:             )?;
1252:             let lease_id = format!("{task_id}/{step_id}/lease/{lease_seq}");
1253: 
1254:             let inserted = tx.execute(
1255:                 "INSERT INTO leases
1256:                  (lease_id, task_id, step_id, worker_id, acquired_generation, expires_at_generation, state)
1257:                  SELECT ?1, ?2, ?3, 'worker-scheduler', ?4, ?5, 'active'
1258:                  WHERE NOT EXISTS (
1259:                      SELECT 1 FROM leases
1260:                      WHERE task_id = ?2 AND step_id = ?3 AND state = 'active'
1261:                  )",
1262:                 params![lease_id, task_id, step_id, current_generation, current_generation + 2],
1263:             )?;
1264: 
1265:             if inserted == 0 {
1266:                 continue;
1267:             }
1268: 
1269:             tx.execute(
1270:                 "UPDATE step_status
1271:                  SET status = 'dispatched'
1272:                  WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND status = 'ready'",
1273:                 params![task_id, step_id],
1274:             )?;
1275: 
1276:             let causal_unit_id = current_generation + 1;
1277: 
1278:             let lease_payload = json!({
1279:                 "lease_id": lease_id,
1280:                 "worker_id": "worker-scheduler",
1281:                 "acquired_generation": current_generation,
1282:                 "expires_at_generation": current_generation + 2
1283:             });
1284:             let lease_payload_str = canonical_json(&lease_payload)?;
1285: 
1286:             tx.execute(
1287:                 "INSERT INTO event_log
1288:                  (system_generation, causal_unit_id, sequence_in_unit, task_id, step_id, event_type, payload, logical_generation)
1289:                  VALUES (?1, ?2, 0, ?3, ?4, 'LEASE_ACQUIRED', ?5, ?6)",
1290:                 params![
1291:                     causal_unit_id,
1292:                     causal_unit_id,
1293:                     task_id,
1294:                     step_id,
1295:                     lease_payload_str,
1296:                     causal_unit_id
1297:                 ],
1298:             )?;
1299: 
1300:             let dispatch_payload = json!({
1301:                 "lease_id": lease_id,
1302:                 "worker_id": "worker-scheduler"
1303:             });
1304:             let dispatch_payload_str = canonical_json(&dispatch_payload)?;
1305: 
1306:             tx.execute(
1307:                 "INSERT INTO event_log
1308:                  (system_generation, causal_unit_id, sequence_in_unit, task_id, step_id, event_type, payload, logical_generation)
1309:                  VALUES (?1, ?2, 1, ?3, ?4, 'STEP_DISPATCHED', ?5, ?6)",
1310:                 params![
1311:                     causal_unit_id,
1312:                     causal_unit_id,
1313:                     task_id,
1314:                     step_id,
1315:                     dispatch_payload_str,
1316:                     causal_unit_id
1317:                 ],
1318:             )?;
1319: 
1320:             println!("DISPATCHED: {}", step_id);
1321:         }
1322: 
1323:         tx.commit()?;
1324: 
1325:         let mut status: BTreeMap<String, String> = BTreeMap::new();
1326:         let conn2 = self.conn()?;
1327:         let mut stmt = conn2.prepare(
1328:             "SELECT step_id, status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY step_id",
1329:         )?;
1330:         let rows = stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
1331:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
1332:         })?;
1333: 
1334:         for row in rows.filter_map(|r| r.ok()) {
1335:             status.insert(row.0, row.1);
1336:         }
1337: 
1338:         println!("STEP_STATUS: {:?}", status);
1339:         Ok(status)
1340:     }
1341: 
1342:     fn get_next_ready_step(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {
1343:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1344:         for step_id in ordered_step_ids(&conn, task_id)? {
1345:             let status: Option<String> = conn
1346:                 .query_row(
1347:                     "SELECT status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2",
1348:                     params![task_id, step_id],
1349:                     |r| r.get(0),
1350:                 )
1351:                 .optional()?;
1352: 
1353:             if !matches!(status.as_deref(), Some("ready") | Some("dispatched")) {
1354:                 continue;
1355:             }
1356: 
1357:             let active_lease: Option<i64> = conn
1358:                 .query_row(
1359:                     "SELECT 1 FROM leases WHERE task_id = ?1 AND step_id = ?2 AND state = 'active' LIMIT 1",
1360:                     params![task_id, step_id],
1361:                     |r| r.get(0),
1362:                 )
1363:                 .optional()?;
1364: 
1365:             if active_lease.is_none() {
1366:                 return Ok(Some(step_id));
1367:             }
1368:         }
1369:         Ok(None)
1370:     }

1372:     fn get_current_status_map(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
1373:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1374:         let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
1375:         let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
1376:             "SELECT step_id, status FROM step_status WHERE task_id = ?1 ORDER BY step_id",
1377:         )?;
1378:         let rows = stmt.query_map([task_id], |r| {
1379:             Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
1380:         })?;
1381: 
1382:         for row in rows.filter_map(|r| r.ok()) {
1383:             out.insert(row.0, row.1);
1384:         }
1385:         Ok(out)
1386:     }

1393:     fn seed_demo_leases(&self, task_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
1394:         let conn = self.conn()?;
1395:         let current_generation: i64 = conn
1396:             .query_row(

121:     pub async fn build_from_task_llm(input: &TaskInput) -> Result<Vec<Step>> {
122:         let normalized = input.detail().unwrap_or("");
123: 
124:         if normalized.is_empty() {
125:             return Ok(Self::build_steps(input));
126:         }
127: 
128:         let deterministic = Self::build_steps(input);
129: 
130:         let prompt = format!(
131:             "You are improving an EXISTING Rust kernel planner. \
132: Refine the provided draft plan into short implementation steps. \
133: Keep the same scope, stay grounded in the existing code, and return one step per line. \
134: No numbering, no bullets, no commentary.\n\nTask: {}\n\nDraft plan:\n{}",
135:             normalized,
136:             deterministic

146: 
147:         for kind in parsed {

149:             if !steps.iter().any(|existing| existing == &step) {
150:                 steps.push(step);
151:             }
152: 
153:             if steps.len() == deterministic.len() {
154:                 break;
155:             }
156:         }
157: 
158:         Ok(validate_steps(steps))
159:     }
160: }

88: fn extract_path(desc: &str) -> Option<String> {
89:     let parts: Vec<&str> = desc.split_whitespace().collect();
90:     for (i, &word) in parts.iter().enumerate() {
91:         let wl = word.to_lowercase();
92:         if (wl == "file" || wl == "read" || wl == "write" || wl == "create") && i + 1 < parts.len()
93:         {
94:             let next = parts[i + 1].trim_matches(|c| c == '\'' || c == '"' || c == '`');
95:             let next_lower = next.to_lowercase();
96:             if next_lower == "file" && i + 2 < parts.len() {
97:                 let after_file = parts[i + 2].trim_matches(|c| c == '\'' || c == '"' || c == '`');
98:                 if !after_file.is_empty() {
99:                     return Some(after_file.to_string());
100:                 }
101:             } else if !next.is_empty() && next_lower != "file" {
102:                 return Some(next.to_string());
103:             }
104:         }
105:     }
106:     None
107: }
108: 
109: fn extract_content(desc: &str) -> Option<String> {
110:     let lower = desc.to_lowercase();
111:     if let Some(idx) = lower.find(" with ") {
112:         let content = desc[idx + 6..]
113:             .trim()
114:             .trim_matches(|c| c == '\'' || c == '"' || c == '`');
115:         return Some(content.to_string());
116:     }
117:     None
118: }

120: fn extract_command(desc: &str) -> Option<String> {
121:     let lower = desc.to_lowercase();
122:     if let Some(idx) = lower.find("command ") {
123:         return Some(desc[idx + 8..].to_string());
124:     }
125:     if let Some(idx) = lower.find("run ") {
126:         return Some(desc[idx + 4..].to_string());
127:     }
128:     if let Some(idx) = lower.find("execute ") {
129:         return Some(desc[idx + 8..].to_string());
130:     }
131:     None
132: }

190:                 || detail_lower.contains("create file")
191:             {
192:                 let path = extract_path(raw_detail).unwrap_or_else(|| "dummy.txt".to_string());
193:                 let content =
194:                     extract_content(raw_detail).unwrap_or_else(|| "dummy content".to_string());
195:                 (
196:                     crate::execution_abi::primitives::PrimitiveKind::Write,
197:                     serde_json::json!({
198:                         "path": path,
199:                         "content": content,
200:                         "detail": step.detail.clone(),
201:                         "step_kind": format!("{:?}", step.kind)
202:                     }),
203:                 )
204:             } else if detail_lower.contains("run command")
205:                 || detail_lower.contains("run ")
206:                 || detail_lower.contains("execute ")
207:             {
208:                 let cmd = extract_command(raw_detail).unwrap_or_else(|| "echo 'hello'".to_string());
209:                 (
210:                     crate::execution_abi::primitives::PrimitiveKind::Compute,
211:                     serde_json::json!({
212:                         "command": cmd,
213:                         "detail": step.detail.clone(),
214:                         "step_kind": format!("{:?}", step.kind)
215:                     }),
216:                 )
217:             } else {
218:                 (
219:                     primitive_kind,
220:                     serde_json::json!({
221:                         "requires_llm": requires_llm,
222:                         "operation": if is_analyze { "semantic_embedding" } else { "none" },
223:                         "detail": step.detail.clone(),
224:                         "step_kind": format!("{:?}", step.kind)
225:                     }),
226:                 )
227:             };
228: 
229:         let primitive = Some(crate::execution_abi::primitives::PrimitiveSpec {
230:             id: crate::execution_abi::primitives::PrimitiveId(step_id.clone()),
231:             kind: prim_kind,
232:             payload: prim_payload,
233:         });
234: 
235:         let inputs = match step.kind {
236:             StepKind::AnalyzeTask => vec!["task_description".to_string()],
237:             StepKind::PlanExecution => vec!["analysis_seed".to_string()],
238:             StepKind::ExecuteChanges => vec!["execution_plan".to_string()],
239:             _ => vec![],
240:         };
241:         let outputs = match step.kind {
242:             StepKind::AnalyzeTask => vec!["analysis_seed".to_string()],
243:             StepKind::PlanExecution => vec!["execution_plan".to_string()],
244:             StepKind::ExecuteChanges => vec!["changes_committed".to_string()],
245:             _ => vec![],
246:         };
247:         let metadata = serde_json::json!({
248:             "step_kind": format!("{:?}", step.kind)
249:         });
250: 
251:         steps_specs.push(crate::exec_spec::StepSpec {
252:             step_id: step_id.clone(),
253:             required_capability,
254:             detail: step.detail.clone(),
255:             primitive,
256:             constraints: vec![constraint],
257:             artifact_requirements: vec![],
258:             inputs,
259:             outputs,
260:             metadata,
261:         });
262: 
263:         if i > 0 {
264:             let prev_slug = match steps[i - 1].kind {
265:                 StepKind::TightenPlannerPrompt => "tighten_planner_prompt",
266:                 StepKind::NormalizePlannerOutput => "normalize_planner_output",
267:                 StepKind::AddLlmFallbackHandling => "add_llm_fallback_handling",
268:                 StepKind::AddPlannerTestCoverage => "add_planner_test_coverage",
269:                 StepKind::ValidatePlannerOutput => "validate_planner_output",
270:                 StepKind::AnalyzeTask => "analyze_task",
271:                 StepKind::PlanExecution => "plan_execution",
272:                 StepKind::ExecuteChanges => "execute_changes",
273:                 StepKind::ReadRepository => "read_repository",
274:                 StepKind::LocateBug => "locate_bug",
275:                 StepKind::PatchCode => "patch_code",
276:                 StepKind::RunTests => "run_tests",
277:                 StepKind::ValidatePatch => "validate_patch",
278:             };
279:             let prev_id = format!("{:02}_{}", i - 1, prev_slug);
280:             transitions.push(crate::exec_spec::TransitionRule {
281:                 step_id,
282:                 depends_on: vec![prev_id],
283:             });
284:         }
285:     }
286: 
287:     let mut dependencies = Vec::new();
288:     for t in &transitions {
289:         dependencies.push(crate::exec_spec::Dependency {
290:             step_id: t.step_id.clone(),
291:             depends_on: t.depends_on.clone(),
292:         });
293:     }
294: 
295:     crate::exec_spec::ExecSpec::new(
296:         1,
297:         steps_specs,
298:         transitions,
299:         dependencies,
300:         vec![],
301:         std::collections::BTreeMap::new(),
302:     )
303: }

305: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
306: pub enum WorkerCapability {
307:     Planner,
308:     Executor,
309:     Verifier,
310:     LegacyGeneric,
311: }
312: 
313: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
314: pub struct StepSpec {
315:     pub kind: StepKind,
316:     pub required_capability: WorkerCapability,
317: }
318: 
319: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
320: pub enum StepOutcome {
321:     Success,
322:     RetryableFailure,
323:     TerminalFailure,
324:     Blocked,
325: }
326: 
327: pub fn terminal_outcome(outcome: &StepOutcome) -> bool {
328:     matches!(outcome, StepOutcome::Success | StepOutcome::TerminalFailure)
329: }
330:

365:     &["Success", "RetryableFailure", "TerminalFailure", "Blocked"]
366: }

370:     &[
371:         "task.created",

374:         "task.succeeded",
375:         "task.failed",
376:         "task.blocked",
377:     ]

379: 
380: /// Advisory-only capability hint from the static workflow contract.
381: /// Runtime execution is lease-authorized; this mapping is metadata, not an execution guard.
382: #[allow(dead_code)]
383: pub fn advisory_capability_for_step(step_kind: &StepKind) -> WorkerCapability {
384:     required_capability_for_step(step_kind)
385: }
386: 
387: const GENERIC_FLOW: &[(StepKind, WorkerCapability)] = &[
388:     (StepKind::AnalyzeTask, WorkerCapability::Planner),
389:     (StepKind::PlanExecution, WorkerCapability::Planner),
390:     (StepKind::ExecuteChanges, WorkerCapability::Executor),
391: ];
392: 
393: const PLANNER_HARDENING_FLOW: &[(StepKind, WorkerCapability)] = &[
394:     (StepKind::TightenPlannerPrompt, WorkerCapability::Planner),
395:     (StepKind::NormalizePlannerOutput, WorkerCapability::Planner),
396:     (StepKind::AddLlmFallbackHandling, WorkerCapability::Planner),
397:     (StepKind::AddPlannerTestCoverage, WorkerCapability::Planner),
398:     (StepKind::ValidatePlannerOutput, WorkerCapability::Verifier),
399: ];
400: 
401: const CODEFIX_FLOW: &[(StepKind, WorkerCapability)] = &[
402:     (StepKind::ReadRepository, WorkerCapability::Planner),
403:     (StepKind::LocateBug, WorkerCapability::Planner),
404:     (StepKind::PatchCode, WorkerCapability::Executor),
405:     (StepKind::RunTests, WorkerCapability::Executor),
406:     (StepKind::ValidatePatch, WorkerCapability::Verifier),
407: ];
408: 
409: fn flow_table(task_class: TaskClass) -> &'static [(StepKind, WorkerCapability)] {
410:     match task_class {
411:         TaskClass::Generic => GENERIC_FLOW,
412:         TaskClass::PlannerHardening => PLANNER_HARDENING_FLOW,
413:         TaskClass::CodeFix => CODEFIX_FLOW,
414:     }
415: }
416: 
417: pub fn required_capability_for_step(step_kind: &StepKind) -> WorkerCapability {
418:     for (kind, capability) in GENERIC_FLOW
419:         .iter()
420:         .chain(PLANNER_HARDENING_FLOW.iter())
421:         .chain(CODEFIX_FLOW.iter())
422:     {
423:         if kind == step_kind {
424:             return *capability;
425:         }
426:     }
427: 
428:     panic!("missing capability mapping for step kind")
429: }
430: 
431: pub fn task_class_to_flow(task_class: TaskClass) -> Vec<StepSpec> {
432:     flow_table(task_class)
433:         .iter()
434:         .map(|(kind, capability)| StepSpec {
435:             kind: kind.clone(),
436:             required_capability: *capability,
437:         })
438:         .collect()
439: }
440: 
441: pub fn step_specs_to_steps(step_specs: &[StepSpec], detail: Option<&str>) -> Vec<Step> {
442:     step_specs
443:         .iter()
444:         .map(|spec| Step {
445:             kind: spec.kind.clone(),
446:             detail: detail.map(|d| d.to_string()),
447:         })
448:         .collect()
449: }

184:                 ));

190:                     right_capsule.state_graph.nodes.len()
191:                 ));
192:             }

195:                     "edges differ (left={}, right={})",
196:                     left_capsule.state_graph.edges.len(),
197:                     right_capsule.state_graph.edges.len()
198:                 ));
199:             }
200:             if reasons.is_empty() {
201:                 "capsules differ".to_string()
202:             } else {
203:                 reasons.join("; ")
204:             }
205:         }
206:         _ => "unknown comparison state".to_string(),

208: 
209:     Ok(CompareCapsulesSummary {
210:         left_capsule_id: left_capsule.capsule_id.clone(),
211:         right_capsule_id: right_capsule.capsule_id.clone(),
